# Recent-validity playbook — from observation week (Jul 5–10 2026)
Authorized fingerprints = executable observation-week ENTRY VARIANTS whose outcome hit TARGET. Reaction strength is learned, not assumed: a tap entry and a strong-displacement entry are separate authorizations and never substitute for one another. Mechanism demonstration only (not statistical proof).

## PB-0001 — ENTRY_ON_DELAYED_DISPLACEMENT (bearish, 1m)
During **ASIA**, when price interacts with a bearish rejection block that was not yet activated (1m), **wait for delayed displacement (2-5 candles), then enter**. Stop at structure invalidation; target the nearest opposing **rb 1m** structure (natural RR 0.5-1.0). Reaction state: **DELAYED_DISPLACEMENT**. Authorized by 1 observation variant(s) (EP-03568-ENTRY_ON_DELAYED_DISPLACEMENT) that hit target — SINGLE_OBSERVATION_PROVISIONAL; valid through 2026-07-17 16:59:00-04:00.
